Let's examine exact KKT and use it for final polish: For a near-optimal, many lags active. We can solve for dual weights p using nonlinear equations: ∑ p_k c_k = C, c_k=C active; and M_p a = λ (constant) on support (fixed-sum gradient). We can update p and a alternately:
- Given a, choose active set and solve for p satisfying M_p a constant and ∑ p_k c_k=C, p≥0. This is a small linear/convex problem: Variables p_q over active Q. Constraints M_p a = λ 1 (Q? n equalities too many) not exactly; instead minimize deviation. We can solve least squares: find p≥0,∑p=1 minimizing variance of M_p a over support and maybe c active equal. Since Q small, we can use scipy least squares with nonnegative? Or multiplicative weights. Then update a via gradient. This is what adaptive dual p aims.

Algorithm with performance-based p:
- Active mask: c_k >= C - tol (or top Q), Q e.g. 64.
- p uniform active.
- For each outer iteration:
   - h=direct_corr(a,p)
   - line search eta; choose best a_new (maybe with several random p? no)
   - If improved: update a; compute conv; C; active; update p: q = c_new restricted; weights maybe p ∝ exp(gamma * q/C); normalize. gamma maybe 1.0. Increase gamma if stagnant.
   - If no improve: reduce eta; update p to target poorly reduced constraints? Could compute old conv and new conv candidate? To know which constraints remain high, use candidate best params maybe. Simpler: increase gamma and enlarge active; maybe random p.
This is a stochastic mirror descent on dual.

We can also periodically compute a "Chebyshev" p by reweighting with c^large power to target peaks.

- p_top1 (Dirac at argmax)
- p_uniform active
- p_violation (c^β)
- p_perf_adapted (maintained)
Pick best among them? This increases evals but may escape. At each outer, we can generate 2-3 directions and evaluate their best eta; choose improvement. This is a species-like approach. Good.

Use direct_corr for each p; Q small. Evaluate direction best by trying 3 eta values, not 10, to save. Since multiple p, total evals moderate.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_active(a, deadline, tag):
 a=normalize(a); best=a; best_sc=score
 p_adapt = None
 eta=0.2
 stagnant=0
 while time<deadline:
   conv=fast_self_conv(a); C=max
   # active indices
   top_q = choose [top 24 or > C*(1-1e-3)] ensuring if close all maybe top_q min 24 max 128
   # define candidate p dicts
   ps=[]
   if p_adapt active same: ps.append(('adapt',p_adapt))
   ps.append(('uni',uniform over active))
   ps.append(('peak', power weights c^2 over active))
   # maybe 'single', top 1
   best_iter=a, sc=best_sc
   for name,p in ps:
      h=direct_corr(a, idx, p)
      if variance small skip
      # try etas: eta*0.5, eta, eta*2 (maybe current best_eta stored)
      for et in ...:
         a_try=step(a,h,et)
         sc=exact_score(a_try)
         if sc<best_iter: update
      # optional if improved, refine with 2 etas around
   if best_iter_sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
       # update adapt p based on new active: p_adapt ∝ c_new^gamma (maybe blend with old)
       a=..., best...
       stagnant=0
       if stagnant improved every few, eta*=1.1
   else:
       stagnant+=1; eta*=0.7; enlarge active; if stagnant>20: random restart/small perturb or break phase
```
Need active indices same for p? p_adapt length Q may need align. Simpler maintain p_adapt as an array over full conv? That would make direct_corr with dense p expensive. Instead maintain p over active list; when active changes, reset p_adapt to power weights.

```
 grad_val = 2*n * ( gradR_c/S^2? Wait R= (1/S^2) L, L=mean smooth.
L = logsumexp(beta c)/beta.
```

```
def sqrt_prefix(t, L):
    a=np.zeros(L); a0=sqrt(t[0]); if a0==0: need handle? assume >0.
    for k in range(1,L):
        s=0.0
        # terms i=1..k-1: 2 a_i a_{k-i}; if k even add a[k/2]^2
        # loop i=1 to k//2
        for i in range(1, k//2+1):
            j=k-i
            if i<j: s += 2*a[i]*a[j]
            else: s += a[i]*a[j] # when i==j? Actually if k even i=k/2 once
        a[k] = (t[k] - s)/(2*a0)
    return a
```

`sqrt_prefix(t, L)` where t length L. But t max maybe 1; if a0 small, division large. For L=2048, okay. We need avoid slow Python loops? Use vector dot.
```
def sqrt_prefix(t, L):
    a=np.zeros(L); a[0]=math.sqrt(max(t[0],1e-12))
    for k in range(1,L):
       if k>1:
          dot = np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])
       else: dot=0.0
       val=(t[k]-dot)/(2*a[0])
       if val < -1e-8: return None? # infeasible
       a[k]=max(val,0.0)
    return a
```
But dot slicing creates views? `a[k-1:0:-1]` okay. Complexity. For L=2048, 2047 dots. Each dot variable length. Good.
This computes root such that first L convolution coeffs equal t if a0 nonzero. But if we clamp negative to 0, no longer exact; for init okay. For infeasible target with negative, discard.

```
def make_targets(L):
  targets=[]
  # flat
  t=np.ones(L); targets.append(t)
  for m in [2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512]:
     for r in [0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9]:
        t=np.ones(L)
        if m>1:
           ramp=np.linspace(r,1.0,m)
           t[:m]=ramp
        else: t[0]=r
        targets.append(t)
  # decaying ramp toward 1: t[k]=1 - beta exp(-k/tau)
  for tau in [16,64,256]: beta in [0.2,0.5,0.8]: t=1-beta*np.exp(-np.arange(L)/tau); ensure t0>=0.1
```

Implement `direct_corr(a, idx, vals)`:
```
n=a.size; h=np.zeros(n)
for k,v in zip(idx,vals):
   if v==0: continue
   lo = k - n + 1
   if lo < 0: lo=0
   hi = k + 1
   if hi > n: hi=n
   start = k - hi + 1
   end = k - lo + 1
   h[lo:hi] += v * a[start:end][::-1]
```
Test with k=2,n=5: valid i=0..2; lo=0 hi=3 start=0 end=3 -> a[::-1]? a[0:3]=[a0,a1,a2] flip [a2,a1,a0]. h[0]=a2,k-i=2-i: i0 a2, i1 a1, i2 a0. good. k=6,n=5 (2n-2=8): lo=2 hi=5 start=2 end=5 -> a[2:5] flip [a4,a3,a2]. i=2 h2=a4 (6-2=4), i3=a3,i4=a2. good.
This allocates flipped copy each loop. Could use `np.flip(a[start:end], out=?)` no. Fine.

```
def corr_pair(a,w): # length n, 2n-1
  n=len(a); m=len(w); M=n+m-1; N=next_pow2(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N); full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]; return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Function `load_parent()`:
```
def load_parent():
  h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
  if h is None: return None
  arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).ravel()
  arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
  arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
  if arr.size<32 or arr.sum()<0.01: return None
  return arr
```

`normalize`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
  a=np.nan_to_num(a,...)
  a=np.clip(a,0,None)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<1e-15: return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size) if a.size else np.array([1.0])
  return a/s
```

```
 if a.size==n: return a.copy()

 x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

Coarse target sweep function top-level:
```
def coarse_target_search(L, deadline, verbose=False):
  targets=[]
  t=np.ones(L); targets.append(t)
  ms=[2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512]
  rs=[0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9]
  for m in ms:
    if m<L:
      for r in rs:
         t=np.ones(L)
         if m>1:
            ramp=np.linspace(r,1.0,m)
            t[:m]=ramp
         else: t[0]=r
         targets.append(t)
  taus=[16,64,256]
  betas=[0.2,0.4,0.7,0.9]
  for tau in taus:
    for beta in betas:
```

```
def optimize_active(a, deadline, tag="", verbose=False, rng=None, other_seeds=None):

    a=normalize_seq(a)

    adapt_idx=(); adapt_p=None; stagnant=0; retry_small=0

    t_start=time.monotonic()

        it += 1

        p_single=np.array([1.0])

        directions = [('adapt', active, p_adapt), ('power', active,p_power), ('uni',active,p_uniform), ('single',single_idx,p_single)]
        # To save, if Q>64 maybe skip uni? no.
```

def get_active(conv, q, rel_tol):
  C=float(np.max(conv))
  if C<=0: return np.array([0],int)
  threshold = C*(1-rel_tol)
  thresh_abs = max(1e-12, threshold)
  mask = conv >= thresh_abs
  cnt=int(mask.sum())
  if cnt < q:
      k=min(q, conv.size)
      idx=np.argpartition(conv, -k)[-k:]
  elif cnt > q*2: # Use q max? We'll cap to q not q*2? choose min(q, cnt? Actually pass q as desired max.
      idx=np.argpartition(conv, -q)[-q:]
  else:
      idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
  if len(idx)<1: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(conv))])
  idx=np.sort(idx).astype(int)
  return idx

   k=min(q, conv.size); idx=argpartition...

def add_init(inits, cap, n, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap: return
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size ==0: return
    if arr.size != n: arr=resample_sequence(arr,n)
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
    mn=float(np.min(arr))
    if mn <0: arr=arr - mn + 0.01
    p=sum
    if s <1e-12: arr=np.ones(n)
    inits.append(arr/s)

 c=fast_self_conv(a)
 cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
 if cmax<0: cmax=0
 return float(2.0*n*cmax/(S*S)), c

```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function search for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The search combines:
  - a coarse "causal square-root" family that directly designs the first-half
    convolution target and recovers a by formal square-root recurrence;
  - large-n exponentiated-gradient optimization with several dual-weight
    directions (adaptive, peak-weighted, single-active);
  - adaptive active-set size, eta, and stagnation restarts;
  - final LSE-LBFGS polishing.

The program assumes evaluate_sequence() is already available in the global
namespace and is used only for final verification.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SCIPY = False


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def load_parent():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size < 64 or float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        return None
    return arr


def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return out.astype(np.float64)


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def score_and_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S)), c


def exact_score(a):
    sc, _ = score_and_conv(a)
    return sc


def direct_corr(a, idx, vals):
    """
    h_i = sum_k vals * a[idx[k] - i] over valid i.
    Efficient when len(idx) is moderate.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for t in range(idx.size):
        k = int(idx[t])
        v = float(vals[t])
        if v == 0.0 or not np.isfinite(v):
            continue
        lo = k - n + 1
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k + 1
        if hi > n:
            hi = n
        if lo >= hi:
            continue
        start = k - hi + 1
        end = k - lo + 1
        h[lo:hi] += v * a[start:end][::-1]
    return h


def corr_pair(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def get_active(conv, q, rel_tol):
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if conv.size == 0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
    C = float(np.max(conv))
    if C <= 0.0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
    q = int(max(1, min(q, conv.size)))
    tol = max(1e-12, C * float(rel_tol))
    mask = conv >= (C - tol)
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    if cnt < q or cnt > q:
        idx = np.argpartition(conv, -q)[-q:]
    else:
        idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(conv))], dtype=np.int64)
    return np.sort(idx)


def apply_step(a, h, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    hmax = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    floor = 1e-18 / float(n)
    base = a + floor
    u = (hmax - h) / hmax
    u = np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    u = np.clip(u, 0.0, 1.0)
    new = base * np.exp(float(eta) * u)
    s = float(np.sum(new))
    if s < 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a.copy()
    return new / s


def perturb(a, rng, strength=0.10):
    p = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    strength = float(strength)
    if not np.isfinite(strength) or strength <= 0.0:
        strength = 0.10
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    if mode == 0:
        p = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        p = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (1.0 / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        k = int(min(65, max(7, n // 512)))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k -= 1
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern /= max(1e-300, float(np.sum(kern)))
        noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode="same")
        noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
        p = p * np.exp(0.25 * strength * noise)
    elif mode == 3:
        conv = fast_self_conv(p)
        if conv.size:
            C = float(np.max(conv))
            if C > 1e-300:
                q = int(min(64, conv.size))
                active = get_active(conv, q, 0.01)
                vals = np.full(active.size, 1.0 / float(active.size))
                h = direct_corr(p, active, vals)
                hmax = float(np.max(h))
                if hmax > 1e-300:
                    p = p * np.exp(-0.5 * strength * h / hmax)
    elif mode == 4:
        nb = int(rng.integers(6, 24))
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        blocks = 1.0 + 0.35 * strength * (rng.random(nb) * 2.0 - 1.0)
        arr = np.repeat(blocks, per)
        if arr.size < n:
            arr = np.concatenate([arr, np.full(n - arr.size, blocks[-1])])
        p = p * arr[:n]
    else:
        p = p * (0.85 + 0.30 * rng.random(n))
    return normalize_seq(p)


def add_init(inits, cap, n, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    mn = float(np.min(arr)) if arr.size else 0.0
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.01
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    inits.append(arr / s)


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, cap=24):
    inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        add_init(inits, cap, n, parent)
        add_init(inits, cap, n, parent[::-1])
    add_init(inits, cap, n, np.ones(n))

    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for pwr in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        arr = (np.arange(n) + 1.0) ** (-float(pwr))
        add_init(inits, cap, n, arr)
        add_init(inits, cap, n, arr[::-1])
        add_init(inits, cap, n, arr + arr[::-1])

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.2, 1.8):
        add_init(inits, cap, n, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
        add_init(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        for alpha in (0.4, 0.8, 1.4):
            add_init(inits, cap, n, 0.7 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + 0.3))
            add_init(inits, cap, n, 0.7 + alpha * np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * f * x)))

    for rate in (1.0, 2.0, 3.5, 6.0, 10.0):
        add_init(inits, cap, n, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)))
        add_init(inits, cap, n, np.exp(-rate * np.abs(x - 0.45)) + 0.02)

    for width in (0.06, 0.12, 0.22):
        for center in (0.18, 0.32, 0.55):
            bumps = np.exp(-(((x - center) / width) ** 2))
            add_init(inits, cap, n, bumps)
            add_init(inits, cap, n, bumps + bumps[::-1])

    Nf = next_pow2(n)
    for _ in range(6):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * float(Nf)
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.2, 3.5)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
        add_init(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.0)) * noise)

    for _ in range(4):
        p = rng.uniform(0.02, 1.0, size=n)
        add_init(inits, cap, n, p)

    return inits


def sqrt_prefix(t, L):
    """
    Given desired first L convolution coefficients t[0:L], compute prefix
    square-root a[0:L] such that (a*a)[0:L] == t, assuming t[0] > 0.
    O(L^2), intended for moderate L (coarse design).
    """
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    a0 = float(t[0])
    if not np.isfinite(a0) or a0 <= 1e-14:
        return None
    a[0] = math.sqrt(a0)
    for k in range(1, L):
        if k > 1:
            s = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k - 1:0:-1]))
        else:
            s = 0.0
        den = 2.0 * a[0]
        if abs(den) < 1e-14:
            return None
        val = (float(t[k]) - s) / den
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return None
        a[k] = val
    return a


def coarse_target_search(L, deadline, top=5):
    L = int(max(128, L))
    targets = []

    t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    targets.append(t)

    for m in (2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512):
        if m >= L:
            continue
        for r in (0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85, 0.95):
            tt = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            ramp = np.linspace(float(r), 1.0, int(m))
            tt[:m] = ramp
            targets.append(tt)

    for tau in (16, 64, 256, 1024):
        for beta in (0.20, 0.45, 0.75, 0.95):
            tt = 1.0 - float(beta) * np.exp(-np.arange(L) / float(tau))
            tt = np.clip(tt, 0.05, 1.0)
            targets.append(tt)

    for m in (4, 16, 64, 256):
        if m >= L:
            continue
        for r in (0.7, 0.9):
            tt = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            idx = np.arange(m)
            tt[:m] = float(r) + (1.0 - float(r)) * (idx / float(m))
            targets.append(tt)

    results = []
    for tt in targets:
        if time.monotonic() > max(t0_global if False else 0.0, deadline) - 2.0:
            break
        tt = np.asarray(tt, dtype=np.float64).ravel()[:L]
        tt = np.nan_to_num(tt, nan=1.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=1.0)
        tt = np.clip(tt, 1e-6, 1.0)
        a = sqrt_prefix(tt, L)
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        neg = a < -1e-7
        if int(np.count_nonzero(neg)) > max(3, L // 100):
            continue
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc, _ = score_and_conv(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            results.append((float(sc), a.copy()))

    if not results:
        fallback = normalize_seq((np.arange(L) + 1.0) ** -0.5)
        return [(exact_score(fallback), fallback)]

    scores = np.asarray([r[0] for r in results], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for i in order[:top]:
        out.append(results[int(i)])
    return out


def optimize_active(a, deadline, tag="", verbose=False, rng=None, other_seeds=None):
    a = normalize_seq(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), a

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    if other_seeds is None:
        other_seeds = []

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc, _ = score_and_conv(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e18

    q = 24
    max_q = min(64, 2 * n)
    rel_tol = 0.004
    eta = 0.25
    gamma = 2.0
    adapt_idx = None
    adapt_p = None
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        it += 1
        conv = fast_self_conv(a)
        C = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0.0
        if C < 0.0:
            C = 0.0
        sc = float(2.0 * n * C)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break

        active = get_active(conv, q, rel_tol)
        Q = int(active.size)
        if Q == 0:
            break
        Csafe = max(C, 1e-300)

        p_power_vals = conv[active] / Csafe
        p_power = p_power_vals ** 6.0
        ss = float(np.sum(p_power))
        if ss <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            p_power = np.full(Q, 1.0 / float(Q))
        else:
            p_power = p_power / ss

        if adapt_idx is not None:
            adapt_idx = np.asarray(adapt_idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
            same = (len(adapt_idx) == Q) and bool(np.array_equal(adapt_idx, active))
        else:
            same = False
        if same:
            adapt_p = np.asarray(adapt_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if adapt_p.size != Q:
                adapt_p = p_power.copy()
        else:
            adapt_p = p_power.copy()
            adapt_idx = active.copy()

        idx1 = active[np.argmax(conv[active])]
        idx1 = np.array([int(idx1)], dtype=np.int64)

        directions = []
        directions.append(("adapt", active, adapt_p))
        directions.append(("power", active, p_power))
        directions.append(("single", idx1, np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)))

        best_dir_a = None
        best_dir_sc = sc
        best_dir_conv = None
        best_name = ""

        for name, idx, p in directions:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            h = direct_corr(a, idx, p)
            hmax = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-18:
                continue
            etas = [max(1e-5, eta * 0.5), max(1e-5, eta), max(1e-5, eta * 2.0)]
            for e in etas:
                a_try = apply_step(a, h, e)
                sc_try, conv_try = score_and_conv(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_dir_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_dir_sc = float(sc_try)
                    best_dir_a = a_try.copy()
                    best_dir_conv = conv_try.copy()
                    best_name = name

        if best_dir_a is not None and best_dir_sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
            a = best_dir_a
            best_sc = best_dir_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_conv = best_dir_conv
            stagnant = 0
            eta = min(1.6, eta * 1.12)

            new_active = get_active(best_conv, q, rel_tol)
            Cnew = float(np.max(best_conv)) if best_conv.size else Csafe
            Cnew = max(Cnew, 1e-300)
            pn = best_conv[new_active] / Cnew
            pn = pn ** gamma
            sn = float(np.sum(pn))
            if sn <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sn):
                pn = np.full(new_active.size, 1.0 / max(1, new_active.size))
            else:
                pn = pn / sn

            if adapt_idx is not None and len(adapt_idx) == len(new_active) and bool(np.array_equal(adapt_idx, new_active)):
                adapt_p = 0.65 * adapt_p + 0.35 * pn
            else:
                adapt_p = pn
                adapt_idx = new_active.copy()

            if verbose and (it % 25 == 0 or it <= 5):
                print("  [{}] it={} score={:.10f} eta={:.3f} q={} stale0".format(
                    tag, it, best_sc, eta, q), flush=True)
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta *= 0.82
            if eta < 0.02:
                eta = 0.20
            q = int(min(max_q, q + 3))
            rel_tol = min(0.025, rel_tol * 1.12)

            if adapt_p is not None and adapt_idx is not None:
                vals = conv[np.asarray(adapt_idx, dtype=np.int64)] / Csafe
                vals = np.clip(vals, 0.0, 1.0)
                vals = (vals ** (gamma + 1.5)).astype(np.float64)
                sv = float(np.sum(vals))
                if sv > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(sv):
                    adapt_p = vals / sv
                else:
                    adapt_p = p_power.copy()

            if stagnant in (8, 18, 30):
                alt = perturb(a, rng, strength=0.035 + 0.012 * (stagnant // 8))
                sc_alt, _ = score_and_conv(alt)
                if np.isfinite(sc_alt) and sc_alt < best_sc:
                    a = alt.copy()
                    best_sc = sc_alt
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    stagnant = 0

            if stagnant > 32:
                if other_seeds:
                    seed_arr = other_seeds.pop(0)
                    seed_arr = normalize_seq(resample_sequence(seed_arr, n))
                    alt_sc, _ = score_and_conv(seed_arr)
                    if np.isfinite(alt_sc):
                        a = seed_arr
                        if alt_sc < best_sc:
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            best_sc = alt_sc
                        stagnant = 0
                        eta = 0.28
                        q = max(24, min(max_q, q // 2))
                        rel_tol = 0.004
                        adapt_idx = None
                        adapt_p = None
                        if verbose:
                            print("  [{}] restart from other seed, score={:.10f}".format(tag, best_sc), flush=True)
                else:
                    a = perturb(best_a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.12)))
                    stagnant = 0
                    eta = 0.28
                    q = max(24, min(max_q, q // 2))
                    rel_tol = 0.004
                    adapt_idx = None
                    adapt_p = None

    best_sc, _ = score_and_conv(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e18
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def lse_fun(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e4)
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0 or S < 1e-12:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    y = x / S
    c = fast_self_conv(y)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 1.0:
        beta = 1.0
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(np.max(z)) if c.size else 0.0
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    se = float(np.sum(ez))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    logL = (zmax + math.log(se)) / beta
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * logL
    w = ez / se
    if w.size:
        rel_thresh = 1e-10 * float(np.max(w))
        nz = np.nonzero(w > rel_thresh)[0]
        if 0 < nz.size < 1024:
            h = direct_corr(y, nz, w[nz])
        else:
            h = corr_pair(y, w)
    else:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad_norm = 4.0 * float(n) * (h - logL * y)
    grad = grad_norm / S
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e8, neginf=-1e8)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e8, 1e8)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def polish_lbfgs(a, deadline, verbose=False):
    if not HAS_SCIPY:
        return exact_score(a), a
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return exact_score(a), a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e18

    betas = (20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0)
    if n >= 32768:
        beta_max = 300.0
    elif n >= 8192:
        beta_max = 1200.0
    else:
        beta_max = 4000.0

    for beta in betas:
        if beta > beta_max:
            break
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        if n <= 2048:
            cap = 500
        elif n <= 8192:
            cap = 220
        elif n <= 16384:
            cap = 120
        else:
            cap = 70
        maxfun = int(min(cap, max(25, rem / 1.5)))
        bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * n
        opts = {
            "ftol": 1e-14,
            "gtol": 1e-10,
            "maxiter": int(max(15, maxfun // 2)),
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "maxls": 25,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_fun,
                a,
                jac=True,
                args=(float(beta),),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
            a_out = normalize_seq(res.x) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else a
        except Exception:
            a_out = a
        sc = exact_score(a_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            a = a_out.copy()
            if verbose:
                print("  [lse beta={:.0f}] score={:.10f}".format(beta, best_sc), flush=True)

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def prepare_output(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return [0.5] * 64
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    S = float(np.sum(arr))
    if S < 0.01:
        return [0.5] * 64
    arr = arr / S
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0 or float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        return [0.5] * 64
    return [float(v) for v in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 18.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = load_parent()

    overall_a = None
    overall_sc = float("inf")

    if parent is not None:
        pa = normalize_seq(parent)
        sc_pa, _ = score_and_conv(pa)
        if np.isfinite(sc_pa):
            overall_a = pa.copy()
            overall_sc = float(sc_pa)
        print("Parent: n={} score={:.10f}".format(int(parent.size), overall_sc if np.isfinite(overall_sc) else np.inf), flush=True)

    if total_budget >= 1600:
        n_large = 32768
    elif total_budget >= 900:
        n_large = 16384
    else:
        n_large = 8192

    # Coarse target-square-root search.
    coarse_time = int(min(180, max(60, 0.07 * total_budget)))
    coarse_dead = t_start + coarse_time
    Lc = 2048 if total_budget >= 1500 else 1024
    try:
        coarse_top = coarse_target_search(Lc, coarse_dead, top=4)
    except Exception:
        coarse_top = []
    for sc_c, a_c in coarse_top[:1]:
        if sc_c < overall_sc:
            overall_sc = float(sc_c)
            overall_a = a_c.copy()

    seeds = []

    if parent is not None:
        sp = normalize_seq(resample_sequence(parent, n_large))
        scp, _ = score_and_conv(sp)
        if np.isfinite(scp):
            seeds.append(("parent", sp, float(scp)))
            if scp < overall_sc:
                overall_a = sp.copy()
                overall_sc = float(scp)

    for i, (sc_c, a_c) in enumerate(coarse_top[:3]):
        sp = normalize_seq(resample_sequence(a_c, n_large))
        scp, _ = score_and_conv(sp)
        if np.isfinite(scp):
            seeds.append(("coarse{}".format(i), sp, float(scp)))
            if scp < overall_sc:
                overall_a = sp.copy()
                overall_sc = float(scp)

    if parent is None or len(seeds) < 3:
        inits = generate_inits(n_large, rng, parent, cap=14)
        init_recs = []
        for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
            sci, _ = score_and_conv(init)
            if np.isfinite(sci):
                init_recs.append((float(sci), init, idx))
        if init_recs:
            scores = np.asarray([r[0] for r in init_recs], dtype=np.float64)
            order = np.argsort(scores)
            for pos in order[:4]:
                pos = int(pos)
                sci, init, ino = init_recs[pos]
                dup = False
                for nn, aa, ss in seeds:
                    if int(aa.size) == int(init.size) and float(np.max(np.abs(np.asarray(aa) - init))) < 1e-7:
                        dup = True
                        break
                if not dup:
                    seeds.append(("init{}".format(ino), init, float(sci)))
                    if sci < overall_sc:
                        overall_a = init.copy()
                        overall_sc = float(sci)

    if not seeds:
        u = np.ones(n_large, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_large)
        seeds.append(("uniform", u, exact_score(u)))

    parent_seed = None
    other_seeds_items = []
    for nm, arr, sc in seeds:
        if nm.startswith("parent") and parent_seed is None:
            parent_seed = (arr, sc)
        else:
            other_seeds_items.append((nm, arr, sc))

    if other_seeds_items:
        scores = np.asarray([it[2] for it in other_seeds_items], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        other_seeds_items = [other_seeds_items[int(i)] for i in order]

    final_seeds = []
    if parent_seed is not None:
        final_seeds.append(parent_seed)
    for item in other_seeds_items:
        if len(final_seeds) >= 4:
            break
        final_seeds.append(item)

    if not final_seeds:
        u = np.ones(n_large, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_large)
        final_seeds = [(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), exact_score(u))]

    reserve = 520.0 if total_budget >= 1800 else 300.0
    main_dead = deadline - reserve
    if main_dead < t_start + 90.0:
        main_dead = deadline - 60.0

    main_final = main_dead - t_start
    weights_all = [0.55, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10]
    k = len(final_seeds)
    weights = weights_all[:k]
    wsum = float(sum(weights))

    for i in range(k):
        if time.monotonic() > main_dead - 20.0:
            break
        chunk_dead = t_start + main_final * (float(sum(weights[:i + 1])) / wsum)
        if chunk_dead - time.monotonic() < 60.0:
            chunk_dead = main_dead
        arr, sc0 = final_seeds[i]
        tag = "seed{}".format(i)
        others = []
        for j in range(k):
            if j == i:
                continue
            others.append(np.asarray(final_seeds[j][0], dtype=np.float64).copy())
        try:
            sc_opt, a_opt = optimize_active(
                arr,
                deadline=chunk_dead,
                tag=tag,
                verbose=True,
                rng=rng,
                other_seeds=others,
            )
        except Exception:
            sc_opt, a_opt = exact_score(arr), arr
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < overall_sc:
            overall_sc = float(sc_opt)
            overall_a = np.asarray(a_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print("Seed {} improved overall to {:.10f}".format(i, overall_sc), flush=True)

    if overall_a is None:
        overall_a = np.ones(n_large, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_large)
        overall_sc = exact_score(overall_a)

    # L-BFGS polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80 and HAS_SCIPY:
        try:
            sc_l, a_l = polish_lbfgs(overall_a, deadline=deadline - 70, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_l) and sc_l < overall_sc:
                overall_sc = float(sc_l)
                overall_a = np.asarray(a_l, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final active polish.
    fin_dead = deadline - 6.0
    if (fin_dead - time.monotonic()) > 50:
        try:
            sc_f, a_f = optimize_active(
                overall_a,
                deadline=fin_dead,
                tag="final",
                verbose=False,
                rng=rng,
                other_seeds=[],
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < overall_sc:
                overall_sc = float(sc_f)
                overall_a = np.asarray(a_f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = prepare_output(overall_a)
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if (not np.isfinite(verified)) or verified > (overall_sc + 1e-7):
        if parent is not None:
            fb = prepare_output(parent)
            try:
                vfb = float(evaluate_sequence(fb))
            except Exception:
                vfb = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(vfb):
                out = fb
                verified = vfb
        if (not np.isfinite(verified)) or verified > (overall_sc + 1e-5):
            out = [0.5] * 64

    print(
        "DONE: internal={:.10f} verified={:.10f} n={} time={:.1f}s".format(
            overall_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
        ),
        flush=True,
    )
    return out
```
